<!-- mission -->
<section class="full-width mission transparent-logo">
<div class="padded">
<h2 class="title-line">Our Mission</h2>
<p>To promote, protect and preserve hunting and the shooting sports. Formed in 1961, NSSF has a membership of thousands of manufacturers, distributors, firearms retailers, shooting ranges, sportsmen’s organizations and publishers nationwide. </p>
</div>

<span class="date">January 21 - 24, 2020</span><h3>2020 SHOT Show<sup style="font-size:.5em; letter-spacing: 0; vertical-align: super;">®</sup></h3><div class="content">The SHOT Show is the largest and most comprehensive trade show for the shooting sports, hunting and law enforcement industries.</div><a class="button" href="https://shotshow.org/" target="_blank">Learn more</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</section>

<h3>The Educational Resource for the Firearms Industry</h3>
<p>
										NSSF’s SHOT University Online is a one-stop e-learning platform for firearms retailers and ranges and NSSF Members have free access to all online educational content. 										
									</p>
